<div class="b-steps-container" id="steps">
<div class="container">
<div class="row">
<div class="title_text_default col-md-12 col-sm-16 col-xs-16 col-md-offset-2 col-sm-offset-0 col-xs-offset-0 col-xxs-16 col-xxs-offset-0 col-lg- col-lg-offset-">
<div class="title_text_default_wrapper">
<h2 class="text-center title_text_default__title b-steps-container__title">Быстрый запуск онлайн‑обучения за 3 простых шага</h2> <div class="clearfix"></div>
</div></div>
</div> </div>
<div class="container-fluid b-steps-container__items-wrapper">
<div class="b-steps-container__item lazy_background">
<div class="row">
<div class="col-xs-14 col-xs-offset-1 col-sm-12 col-sm-offset-2 col-md-8 col-md-offset-0">
<div class="b-steps-container__item-description b-steps-container__item-animation b-steps-container__item-animation-1">
<div class="b-steps-container__item-number">
<span class="b-steps-container__item-step-number">1</span>
<div class="b-steps-container__item-circles"></div>
</div>
<span class="b-steps-container__item-step-title">Создайте аккаунт</span>
<div class="b-steps-container__item-content b-steps-container__item-content_first">
<p class="b-steps-container__item-step-text">
                                Чтобы начать обучение, достаточно <a class="b-steps-container__item-step-link b-steps-container__item-step-text-link" href="/ispring-learn/get-free-trial" rel="nofollow" target="_blank" title="">зарегистрироваться в iSpring</a>.
                                Сразу после этого вы сможете:
                            </p>
<ul class="b-steps-container__list">
<li class="b-steps-container__item-step-text b-steps-container__list-item">Загрузить учебные материалы</li>
